Parse generator cost data\n    gencost = []\n    for gc in data['gencost']:\n        model = int(gc[0])\n        startup = gc[1]\n        shutdown = gc[2]\n        ncost = int(gc[3])\n        cost_coeffs = gc[4:]\n        gencost.append({\n            'model': model,\n            'startup': startup,\n            'shutdown': shutdown,\n            'ncost': ncost,\n            'cost': cost_coeffs\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'base_mva': base_mva,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n        'gencost': gencost\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_data: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC-OPF using the mathematical model from math-model.md\n    \"\"\"\n    base_mva = network_data['base_mva']\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    gencost = network_data['gencost']\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    n_branches = len(branches)\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n    gen_bus_to_idx = {g['gen_bus']: i for i, g in enumerate(generators)}\n\n    # Identify reference bus (type == 3)\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n        if b['bus_type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        ref_bus_idx = 0  # Default to first bus\n\n    # Variables\n    # Bus voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = cp.Variable(n_buses, pos=True, name='vm')  # Voltage magnitude\n    va = cp.Variable(n_buses, name='va')  # Voltage angle\n\n    # Generator real and reactive power\n    pg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='pg')  # Real power\n    qg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='qg')  # Reactive power\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle constraint: angle = 0\n    constraints.append(va[ref_bus_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] == 1:  # Generator is on\n            constraints.append(pg[i] >= gen['pmin'])\n            constraints.append(pg[i] <= gen['pmax'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] >= gen['qmin'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] <= gen['qmax'])\n        else:\n            # Generator is off\n            constraints.append(pg[i] == 0)\n            constraints.append(qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        constraints.append(vm[i] >= bus['vmin'])\n        constraints.append(vm[i] <= bus['vmax'])\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        # Get generators connected to this bus\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        # Calculate power injection from generators\n        pg_sum = cp.sum([pg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = cp.sum([qg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        # Calculate power injection from loads (negative values)\n        pd = bus['pd']\n        qd = bus['qd']\n\n        # Calculate power flow through branches\n        pf_from = 0.0  # Real power flowing out from bus\n        qf_from = 0.0  # Reactive power flowing out from bus\n\n        pf_to = 0.0    # Real power flowing into bus\n        qf_to = 0.0    # Reactive power flowing into bus\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['status'] != 1:  # Branch is out of service\n                continue\n\n            f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n            t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n            if f_bus_idx == i:\n                # This bus is the \"from\" bus\n                r = br['br_r']\n                x = br['br_x']\n                b = br['br_b']\n                tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n                angle_shift = br['angle'] * math.pi / 180\n\n                # Calculate branch admittance\n                y = 1.0 / (r + 1j * x)\n                y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n                # Voltage at buses\n                vf = vm[f_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n                vt = vm[t_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n                # Apply transformer\n                if tap != 1.0:\n                    vf = vf / tap\n\n                # Complex power flow from f to t\n                s_flow = vf * cp.conj(vf * (y + y_shunt)) - vf * cp.conj(vt * y)\n                pf_from += cp.real(s_flow)\n                qf_from += cp.imag(s_flow)\n\n            elif t_bus_idx == i:\n                # This bus is the \"to\" bus\n                r = br['br_r']\n                x = br['br_x']\n                b = br['br_b']\n                tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n\n                y = 1.0 / (r + 1j * x)\n                y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n                f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n                t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n                vf = vm[f_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n                vt = vm[t_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n                if tap != 1.0:\n                    vf = vf / tap\n\n                # Complex power flow from t to f (reverse direction)\n                s_flow = vt * cp.conj(vt * (y + y_shunt)) - vt * cp.conj(vf * y)\n                pf_to += cp.real(s_flow)\n                qf_to += cp.imag(s_flow)\n\n        # Net power injection = generation - load\n        net_p = pg_sum - pd\n        net_q = qg_sum - qd\n\n        # Power balance: net injection = power flowing out\n        constraints.append(net_p == pf_from + pf_to)\n        constraints.append(net_q == qf_from + qf_to)\n\n    # 5. Branch thermal limits\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        y = 1.0 / (r + 1j * x)\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        vf = vm[f_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n        vt = vm[t_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            vf = vf / tap\n\n        s_from = vf * cp.conj(vf * (y + y_shunt)) - vf * cp.conj(vt * y)\n        s_to = vt * cp.conj(vt * (y + y_shunt)) - vt * cp.conj(vf * y)\n\n        # Limit apparent power flow\n        constraints.append(cp.abs(s_from) <= limit)\n        constraints.append(cp.abs(s_to) <= limit)\n\n    # 6. Voltage angle difference limits\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        angle_diff = va[f_bus_idx] - va[t_bus_idx]\n        constraints.append(angle_diff >= math.radians(br['angmin']))\n        constraints.append(angle_diff <= math.radians(br['angmax']))\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'model': 2, 'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n        cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n        if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n            c2, c1, c0 = cost_coeffs[-3], cost_coeffs[-2], cost_coeffs[-1]\n        else:\n            c2, c1, c0 = 0, 1, 0\n\n        # Cost is quadratic in MW (convert to $/hr)\n        total_cost += c2 * (pg[i] ** 2) + c1 * pg[i] + c0\n\n    objective = cp.Minimize(total_cost)\n\n    # Solve the problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=True)\n\n        if problem.status in [\"infeasible\", \"unbounded\"]:\n            status = \"infeasible\"\n        else:\n            status = \"optimal\"\n\n        # Extract results\n        result = {\n            'status': status,\n            'vm': vm.value,\n            'va': va.value,\n            'pg': pg.value,\n            'qg': qg.value,\n            'objective_value': problem.objective.value\n        }\n\n        return result\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error solving AC-OPF: {e}\")\n        return {\n            'status': 'error',\n            'error': str(e)\n        }\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n Parse generator data\n    gen_fields = {\n        'gen_bus': 0, 'pg': 1, 'qg': 2, 'qmax': 3, 'qmin': 4,\n        'vg': 5, 'mbase': 6, 'status': 7, 'pmax': 8, 'pmin': 9\n    }\n\n    generators = []\n    for g in data['gen']:\n        generators.append({\n            'gen_bus': int(g[gen_fields['gen_bus']]),\n            'pg': g[gen_fields['pg']],\n            'qg': g[gen_fields['qg']],\n            'qmax': g[gen_fields['qmax']],\n            'qmin': g[gen_fields['qmin']],\n            'vg': g[gen_fields['vg']],\n            'mbase': g[gen_fields['mbase']],\n            'status': int(g[gen_fields['status']]),\n            'pmax': g[gen_fields['pmax']],\n            'pmin': g[gen_fields['pmin']]\n        })\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    gencost = []\n    for gc in data['gencost']:\n        model = int(gc[0])\n        startup = gc[1]\n        shutdown = gc[2]\n        ncost = int(gc[3])\n        cost_coeffs = gc[4:]\n        gencost.append({\n            'model': model,\n            'startup': startup,\n            'shutdown': shutdown,\n            'ncost': ncost,\n            'cost': cost_coeffs\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'base_mva': base_mva,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n        'gencost': gencost\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_data: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC-OPF using the mathematical model from math-model.md\n    \"\"\"\n    base_mva = network_data['base_mva']\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    gencost = network_data['gencost']\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    n_branches = len(branches)\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n    gen_bus_to_idx = {g['gen_bus']: i for i, g in enumerate(generators)}\n\n    # Identify reference bus (type == 3)\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n        if b['bus_type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        ref_bus_idx = 0  # Default to first bus\n\n    # Variables\n    # Bus voltage magnitudes and angles\n    vm = cp.Variable(n_buses, pos=True, name='vm')  # Voltage magnitude\n    va = cp.Variable(n_buses, name='va')  # Voltage angle\n\n    # Generator real and reactive power\n    pg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='pg')  # Real power\n    qg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='qg')  # Reactive power\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle constraint: angle = 0\n    constraints.append(va[ref_bus_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] == 1:  # Generator is on\n            constraints.append(pg[i] >= gen['pmin'])\n            constraints.append(pg[i] <= gen['pmax'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] >= gen['qmin'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] <= gen['qmax'])\n        else:\n            # Generator is off\n            constraints.append(pg[i] == 0)\n            constraints.append(qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        constraints.append(vm[i] >= bus['vmin'])\n        constraints.append(vm[i] <= bus['vmax'])\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        # Get generators connected to this bus\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        # Calculate power injection from generators\n        pg_sum = cp.sum([pg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = cp.sum([qg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        # Calculate power injection from loads (negative values)\n        pd = bus['pd']\n        qd = bus['qd']\n\n        # Calculate power flow through branches\n        pf_from = 0.0  # Real power flowing out from bus\n        qf_from = 0.0  # Reactive power flowing out from bus\n\n        pf_to = 0.0    # Real power flowing into bus\n        qf_to = 0.0    # Reactive power flowing into bus\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['status'] != 1:  # Branch is out of service\n                continue\n\n            f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n            t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n            if f_bus_idx == i:\n                # This bus is the \"from\" bus\n                r = br['br_r']\n                x = br['br_x']\n                b = br['br_b']\n                tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n                angle_shift = br['angle'] * math.pi / 180\n\n                # Calculate branch admittance\n                y = 1.0 / (r + 1j * x)\n                y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n                # Voltage at buses\n                vf = vm[f_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n                vt = vm[t_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n                # Apply transformer\n                if tap != 1.0:\n                    vf = vf / tap\n\n                # Complex power flow from f to t\n                s_flow = vf * cp.conj(vf * (y + y_shunt)) - vf * cp.conj(vt * y)\n                pf_from += cp.real(s_flow)\n                qf_from += cp.imag(s_flow)\n\n            elif t_bus_idx == i:\n                # This bus is the \"to\" bus\n                r = br['br_r']\n                x = br['br_x']\n                b = br['br_b']\n                tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n\n                y = 1.0 / (r + 1j * x)\n                y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n                f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n                t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n                vf = vm[f_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n                vt = vm[t_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n                if tap != 1.0:\n                    vf = vf / tap\n\n                # Complex power flow from t to f (reverse direction)\n                s_flow = vt * cp.conj(vt * (y + y_shunt)) - vt * cp.conj(vf * y)\n                pf_to += cp.real(s_flow)\n                qf_to += cp.imag(s_flow)\n\n        # Net power injection = generation - load\n        net_p = pg_sum - pd\n        net_q = qg_sum - qd\n\n        # Power balance: net injection = power flowing out\n        constraints.append(net_p == pf_from + pf_to)\n        constraints.append(net_q == qf_from + qf_to)\n\n    # 5. Branch thermal limits\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        y = 1.0 / (r + 1j * x)\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        vf = vm[f_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n        vt = vm[t_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            vf = vf / tap\n\n        s_from = vf * cp.conj(vf * (y + y_shunt)) - vf * cp.conj(vt * y)\n        s_to = vt * cp.conj(vt * (y + y_shunt)) - vt * cp.conj(vf * y)\n\n        # Limit apparent power flow\n        constraints.append(cp.abs(s_from) <= limit)\n        constraints.append(cp.abs(s_to) <= limit)\n\n    # 6. Voltage angle difference limits\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        angle_diff = va[f_bus_idx] - va[t_bus_idx]\n        constraints.append(angle_diff >= math.radians(br['angmin']))\n        constraints.append(angle_diff <= math.radians(br['angmax']))\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'model': 2, 'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n        cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n        if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n            c2, c1, c0 = cost_coeffs[-3], cost_coeffs[-2], cost_coeffs[-1]\n        else:\n            c2, c1, c0 = 0, 1, 0\n\n        # Cost is quadratic in MW (convert to $/hr)\n        total_cost += c2 * (pg[i] ** 2) + c1 * pg[i] + c0\n\n    objective = cp.Minimize(total_cost)\n\n    # Solve the problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=True)\n\n        if problem.status in [\"infeasible\", \"unbounded\"]:\n            status = \"infeasible\"\n        else:\n            status = \"optimal\"\n\n        # Extract results\n        result = {\n            'status': status,\n            'vm': vm.value,\n            'va': va.value,\n            'pg': pg.value,\n            'qg': qg.value,\n            'objective_value': problem.objective.value\n        }\n\n        return result\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error solving AC-OPF: {e}\")\n        return {\n            'status': 'error',\n            'error': str(e)\n        }\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n Parse bus data\n    bus_fields = {\n        'bus_id': 0, 'bus_type': 1, 'pd': 2, 'qd': 3, 'gs': 4, 'bs': 5,\n        'area': 6, 'vm': 7, 'va': 8, 'base_kv': 9, 'zone': 10, 'vmax': 11, 'vmin': 12\n    }\n\n    buses = []\n    for b in data['bus']:\n        buses.append({\n            'bus_id': int(b[bus_fields['bus_id']]),\n            'bus_type': int(b[bus_fields['bus_type']]),\n            'pd': b[bus_fields['pd']],\n            'qd': b[bus_fields['qd']],\n            'gs': b[bus_fields['gs']],\n            'bs': b[bus_fields['bs']],\n            'area': b[bus_fields['area']],\n            'vm': b[bus_fields['vm']],\n            'va': b[bus_fields['va']],\n            'base_kv': b[bus_fields['base_kv']],\n            'zone': b[bus_fields['zone']],\n            'vmax': b[bus_fields['vmax']],\n            'vmin': b[bus_fields['vmin']]\n        })\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    gen_fields = {\n        'gen_bus': 0, 'pg': 1, 'qg': 2, 'qmax': 3, 'qmin': 4,\n        'vg': 5, 'mbase': 6, 'status': 7, 'pmax': 8, 'pmin': 9\n    }\n\n    generators = []\n    for g in data['gen']:\n        generators.append({\n            'gen_bus': int(g[gen_fields['gen_bus']]),\n            'pg': g[gen_fields['pg']],\n            'qg': g[gen_fields['qg']],\n            'qmax': g[gen_fields['qmax']],\n            'qmin': g[gen_fields['qmin']],\n            'vg': g[gen_fields['vg']],\n            'mbase': g[gen_fields['mbase']],\n            'status': int(g[gen_fields['status']]),\n            'pmax': g[gen_fields['pmax']],\n            'pmin': g[gen_fields['pmin']]\n        })\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branch_fields = {\n        'f_bus': 0, 't_bus': 1, 'br_r': 2, 'br_x': 3, 'br_b': 4,\n        'rate_a': 5, 'rate_b': 6, 'rate_c': 7, 'ratio': 8, 'angle': 9,\n        'status': 10, 'angmin': 11, 'angmax': 12\n    }\n\n    branches = []\n    for br in data['branch']:\n        branches.append({\n            'f_bus': int(br[branch_fields['f_bus']]),\n            't_bus': int(br[branch_fields['t_bus']]),\n            'br_r': br[branch_fields['br_r']],\n            'br_x': br[branch_fields['br_x']],\n            'br_b': br[branch_fields['br_b']],\n            'rate_a': br[branch_fields['rate_a']],\n            'ratio': br[branch_fields['ratio']],\n            'angle': br[branch_fields['angle']],\n            'status': int(br[branch_fields['status']]),\n            'angmin': br[branch_fields['angmin']],\n            'angmax': br[branch_fields['angmax']]\n        })\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    gencost = []\n    for gc in data['gencost']:\n        model = int(gc[0])\n        startup = gc[1]\n        shutdown = gc[2]\n        ncost = int(gc[3])\n        cost_coeffs = gc[4:]\n        gencost.append({\n            'model': model,\n            'startup': startup,\n            'shutdown': shutdown,\n            'ncost': ncost,\n            'cost': cost_coeffs\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'base_mva': base_mva,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n        'gencost': gencost\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_data: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC-OPF using rectangular coordinates formulation\n    \"\"\"\n    base_mva = network_data['base_mva']\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    gencost = network_data['gencost']\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Create mappings\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    # Identify reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n        if b['bus_type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Variables in rectangular coordinates\n    # V = e + j*f where e = real part, f = imaginary part\n    e = cp.Variable(n_buses, name='e')  # Real part of voltage\n    f = cp.Variable(n_buses, name='f')  # Imaginary part of voltage\n\n    # Generator real and reactive power\n    pg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='pg')  # Real power\n    qg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='qg')  # Reactive power\n\n    # Helper to calculate voltage magnitude squared\n    def vm_sq(i):\n        return e[i]**2 + f[i]**2\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus constraints\n    constraints.append(e[ref_bus_idx] >= 0.1)  # e > 0\n    constraints.append(f[ref_bus_idx] == 0)    # f = 0 at reference bus\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] == 1:\n            constraints.append(pg[i] >= gen['pmin'])\n            constraints.append(pg[i] <= gen['pmax'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] >= gen['qmin'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] <= gen['qmax'])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(pg[i] == 0)\n            constraints.append(qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds (convert to e^2 + f^2 bounds)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        vmin_sq = bus['vmin']**2\n        vmax_sq = bus['vmax']**2\n        constraints.append(vm_sq(i) >= vmin_sq)\n        constraints.append(vm_sq(i) <= vmax_sq)\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch admittances\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        angle_shift = br['angle'] * math.pi / 180\n\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Apply transformer if not unity tap\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            a = tap * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt) / (abs(a)**2)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y / np.conj(a)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y / a\n        else:\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y\n\n    # Add shunts\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['gs'] != 0 or bus['bs'] != 0:\n            Y[i, i] += (bus['gs'] + 1j * bus['bs'])\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        # Generators connected to this bus\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        pg_sum = cp.sum([pg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = cp.sum([qg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        pd = bus['pd']\n        qd = bus['qd']\n\n        # Complex power injection\n        S_inj = 0 + 0j\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            V_i = e[i] + 1j * f[i]\n            V_j = e[j] + 1j * f[j]\n            S_inj += cp.conj(Y[i, j]) * V_i * (cp.conj(V_i) - cp.conj(V_j))\n\n        # Actually, let's use the standard power flow equations\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            G_ij = cp.real(Y[i, j])\n            B_ij = cp.imag(Y[i, j])\n\n            # P_i = sum(V_i*V_j*(G_ij*cos(theta_i - theta_j) + B_ij*sin(theta_i - theta_j)))\n            # Q_i = sum(V_i*V_j*(G_ij*sin(theta_i - theta_j) - B_ij*cos(theta_i - theta_j)))\n\n            # Using rectangular: P = e_i*(G_ij*e_j - B_ij*f_j) + f_i*(B_ij*e_j + G_ij*f_j)\n            #                 Q = f_i*(G_ij*e_j - B_ij*f_j) - e_i*(B_ij*e_j + G_ij*f_j)\n\n            P_calc += (e[i] * (G_ij * e[j] - B_ij * f[j]) +\n                      f[i] * (B_ij * e[j] + G_ij * f[j]))\n            Q_calc += (f[i] * (G_ij * e[j] - B_ij * f[j]) -\n                      e[i] * (B_ij * e[j] + G_ij * f[j]))\n\n        constraints.append(P_calc == pg_sum - pd)\n        constraints.append(Q_calc == qg_sum - qd)\n\n    # 5. Branch thermal limits (simplified - use loading factor)\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        # Calculate branch current (simplified)\n        # This is approximate - full AC branch equations are complex\n        z_mag = math.sqrt(r**2 + x**2)\n        current_limit = limit / 1.0  # Approximate\n\n        # Voltage drop across branch\n        V_f = e[f_bus_idx] + 1j * f[f_bus_idx]\n        V_t = e[t_bus_idx] + 1j * f[t_bus_idx]\n\n        # Current through branch (approximation)\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            V_f_adj = V_f / tap\n        else:\n            V_f_adj = V_f\n\n        I_f = (V_f_adj - V_t) / (r + 1j * x)\n\n        # Limit |I_f| <= current_limit\n        constraints.append(cp.abs(I_f) <= current_limit)\n\n    # 6. Voltage angle difference limits\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        # Angle difference: atan2(f, e) difference\n        # Approximate using: theta_i - theta_j ≈ (f_i*e_j - e_i*f_j)/(e_i*e_j + f_i*f_j)\n        numerator = f[f_bus_idx] * e[t_bus_idx] - e[f_bus_idx] * f[t_bus_idx]\n        denominator = e[f_bus_idx] * e[t_bus_idx] + f[f_bus_idx] * f[t_bus_idx]\n\n        # Avoid division by zero\n        epsilon = 1e-6\n        angle_diff = cp.atan(numerator / (denominator + epsilon))\n\n        constraints.append(angle_diff >= math.radians(br['angmin']))\n        constraints.append(angle_diff <= math.radians(br['angmax']))\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'model': 2, 'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n        cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n        if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n            c2, c1, c0 = cost_coeffs[-3], cost_coeffs[-2], cost_coeffs[-1]\n        else:\n            c2, c1, c0 = 0, 1, 0\n\n        total_cost += c2 * (pg[i] ** 2) + c1 * pg[i] + c0\n\n    objective = cp.Minimize(total_cost)\n\n    # Solve\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=True, max_iters=1000)\n\n        if problem.status in [\"infeasible\", \"unbounded\"]:\n            status = \"infeasible\"\n        else:\n            status = \"optimal\"\n\n        # Convert rectangular to polar\n        vm = np.sqrt(e.value**2 + f.value**2)\n        va = np.arctan2(f.value, e.value)\n\n        result = {\n            'status': status,\n            'vm': vm,\n            'va': va,\n            'e': e.value,\n            'f': f.value,\n            'pg': pg.value,\n            'qg': qg.value,\n            'objective_value': problem.objective.value\n        }\n\n        return result\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error solving AC-OPF: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return {\n            'status': 'error',\n            'error': str(e)\n        }\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    flows = []\n    e = solution['e']\n    f = solution['f']\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        # Calculate voltages\n        V_f = e[f_bus_idx] + 1j * f[f_bus_idx]\n        V_t = e[t_bus_idx] + 1j * f[t_bus_idx]\n\n        # Apply transformer\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            V_f_adj = V_f / tap\n        else:\n            V_f_adj = V_f\n\n        # Calculate power flow\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        I_f = (V_f_adj - V_t) / z\n        S_from = V_f_adj * np.conj(I_f)\n\n        # Reverse direction\n        I_t = (V_t - V_f_adj) / z\n        S_to = V_t * np.conj(I_t)\n\n        s_from_mva = np.abs(S_from)\n        s_to_mva = np.abs(S_to)\n\n        loading = max(s_from_mva, s_to_mva) / limit * 100 if limit > 0 else 0\n\n        flows.append({\n            'from_bus': br['f_bus'],\n            'to_bus': br['t_bus'],\n            'pf_from': np.real(S_from),\n            'qf_from': np.imag(S_from),\n            'pt_from': np.real(S_to),\n            'qt_from': np.imag(S_to),\n            's_from_mva': s_from_mva,\n            's_to_mva': s_to_mva,\n            'limit': limit,\n            'loading_pct': loading\n        })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef generate_report(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n\n    # Calculate summary statistics\n    total_pg = np.sum(solution['pg'])\n    total_qg = np.sum(solution['qg'])\n    total_pd = np.sum([b['pd'] for b in buses])\n    total_qd = np.sum([b['qd'] for b in buses])\n    total_losses = total_pg - total_pd\n\n    # Calculate feasibility metrics\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0.0\n    max_branch_overload = 0.0\n\n    # Check power balance at each bus\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        pg_sum = np.sum([solution['pg'][idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = np.sum([solution['qg'][idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        mismatch_p = abs(pg_sum - bus['pd'])\n        mismatch_q = abs(qg_sum - bus['qd'])\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, mismatch_p)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, mismatch_q)\n\n        # Check voltage violations\n        vm = solution['vm'][i]\n        if vm > bus['vmax']:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, vm - bus['vmax'])\n        elif vm < bus['vmin']:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, bus['vmin'] - vm)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    flows = calculate_branch_flows(network_data, solution)\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    for flow in flows:\n        if flow['s_from_mva'] > flow['limit']:\n            overload = flow['s_from_mva'] - flow['limit']\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Get most loaded branches\n    most_loaded = sorted(flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)[:10]\n\n    # Format generator data\n    gen_data = 'pg_MW': float(solution['pg'][i]),\n                'qg_MVAr': float(solution['qg'][i]),\n                'pmin_MW': float(gen['pmin']),\n                'pmax_MW': float(gen['pmax']),\n                'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin']),\n                'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'])\n            })\n\n    # Format bus data\n    bus_data = []\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        bus_data.append({\n            'id': bus['bus_id'],\n            'vm_pu': float(solution['vm'][i]),\n            'va_deg': float(np.degrees(solution['va'][i])),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax'])\n        })\n\n    # Format most loaded branches\n    most_loaded_branches = []\n    for flow in most_loaded:\n        most_loaded_branches.append({\n            'from_bus': flow['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': flow['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': float(flow['loading_pct']),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow['s_from_mva']),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow['s_to_mva']),\n            'limit_MVA': float(flow['limit'])\n        })\n\n    # Determine solver status\n    solver_status = python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow (AC-OPF) Solver for ISO Base Case Creation\nUses the mathematical model from math-model.md to find least-cost, AC-feasible operating point\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\nimport math\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load network data from JSON file in MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    base_mva = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    bus_fields = {\n        'bus_id': 0, 'bus_type': 1, 'pd': 2, 'qd': 3, 'gs': 4, 'bs': 5,\n        'area': 6, 'vm': 7, 'va': 8, 'base_kv': 9, 'zone': 10, 'vmax': 11, 'vmin': 12\n    }\n\n    buses = []\n    for b in data['bus']:\n        buses.append({\n            'bus_id': int(b[bus_fields['bus_id']]),\n            'bus_type': int(b[bus_fields['bus_type']]),\n            'pd': b[bus_fields['pd']],\n            'qd': b[bus_fields['qd']],\n            'gs': b[bus_fields['gs']],\n            'bs': b[bus_fields['bs']],\n            'area': b[bus_fields['area']],\n            'vm': b[bus_fields['vm']],\n            'va': b[bus_fields['va']],\n            'base_kv': b[bus_fields['base_kv']],\n            'zone': b[bus_fields['zone']],\n            'vmax': b[bus_fields['vmax']],\n            'vmin': b[bus_fields['vmin']]\n        })\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    gen_fields = {\n        'gen_bus': 0, 'pg': 1, 'qg': 2, 'qmax': 3, 'qmin': 4,\n        'vg': 5, 'mbase': 6, 'status': 7, 'pmax': 8, 'pmin': 9\n    }\n\n    generators = []\n    for g in data['gen']:\n        generators.append({\n            'gen_bus': int(g[gen_fields['gen_bus']]),\n            'pg': g[gen_fields['pg']],\n            'qg': g[gen_fields['qg']],\n            'qmax': g[gen_fields['qmax']],\n            'qmin': g[gen_fields['qmin']],\n            'vg': g[gen_fields['vg']],\n            'mbase': g[gen_fields['mbase']],\n            'status': 't_bus': 1, 'br_r': 2, 'br_x': 3, 'br_b': 4,\n        'rate_a': 5, 'rate_b': 6, 'rate_c': 7, 'ratio': 8, 'angle': 9,\n        'status': 10, 'angmin': 11, 'angmax': 12\n    }\n\n    branches = []\n    for br in data['branch']:\n        branches.append({\n            'f_bus': int(br[branch_fields['f_bus']]),\n            't_bus': int(br[branch_fields['t_bus']]),\n            'br_r': br[branch_fields['br_r']],\n            'br_x': br[branch_fields['br_x']],\n            'br_b': br[branch_fields['br_b']],\n            'rate_a': br[branch_fields['rate_a']],\n            'ratio': br[branch_fields['ratio']],\n            'angle': br[branch_fields['angle']],\n            'status': int(br[branch_fields['status']]),\n            'angmin': br[branch_fields['angmin']],\n            'angmax': br[branch_fields['angmax']]\n        })\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    gencost = []\n    for gc in data['gencost']:\n        model = int(gc[0])\n        startup = gc[1]\n        shutdown = gc[2]\n        ncost = int(gc[3])\n        cost_coeffs = gc[4:]\n        gencost.append({\n            'model': model,\n            'startup': startup,\n            'shutdown': shutdown,\n            'ncost': ncost,\n            'cost': cost_coeffs\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'base_mva': base_mva,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': network_data['base_mva']\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    gencost = network_data['gencost']\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Create mappings\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    # Identify reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n        if b['bus_type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Variables in rectangular coordinates\n    # V = e + j*f where e = real part, f = imaginary part\n    e = cp.Variable(n_buses, name='e')  # Real part of voltage\n    f = cp.Variable(n_buses, name='f')  # Imaginary part of voltage\n\n    # Generator real and reactive power\n    pg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='pg')  # Real power\n    qg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='qg')  # Reactive power\n\n    # Helper to calculate voltage magnitude squared\n    def vm_sq(i):\n        return e[i]**2 + f[i]**2\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus constraints\n    constraints.append(e[ref_bus_idx] >= 0.1)  # e > 0\n    constraints.append(f[ref_bus_idx] == 0)    # f = 0 at reference bus\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] == 1:\n            constraints.append(pg[i] >= gen['pmin'])\n            constraints.append(pg[i] <= gen['pmax'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] >= gen['qmin'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] <= gen['qmax'])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(pg[i] == 0)\n            constraints.append(qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds (convert to e^2 + f^2 bounds)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        vmin_sq = bus['vmin']**2\n        vmax_sq = bus['vmax']**2\n        constraints.append(vm_sq(i) >= vmin_sq)\n        constraints.append(vm_sq(i) <= vmax_sq)\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch admittances\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        angle_shift = br['angle'] * math.pi / 180\n\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Apply transformer if not unity tap\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            a = tap * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt) / (abs(a)**2)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y / np.conj(a)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y / a\n        else:\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y\n\n    # Add shunts\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['gs'] != 0 or bus['bs'] != 0:\n            Y[i, i] += (bus['gs'] + 1j * bus['bs'])\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        # Generators connected to this bus\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        pg_sum = cp.sum([pg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = cp.sum([qg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        pd = bus['pd']\n        qd = bus['qd']\n\n        # Complex power injection\n        S_inj = 0 + 0j\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            V_i = e[i] + 1j * f[i]\n            V_j = e[j] + 1j * f[j]\n            S_inj += cp.conj(Y[i, j]) * V_i * (cp.conj(V_i) - cp.conj(V_j))\n\n        # Actually, let's use the standard power flow equations\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            G_ij = cp.real(Y[i, j])\n            B_ij = cp.imag(Y[i, j])\n\n            # P_i = sum(V_i*V_j*(G_ij*cos(theta_i - theta_j) + B_ij*sin(theta_i - theta_j)))\n            # Q_i = sum(V_i*V_j*(G_ij*sin(theta_i - theta_j) - B_ij*cos(theta_i - theta_j)))\n\n            # Using rectangular: P = e_i*(G_ij*e_j - B_ij*f_j) + f_i*(B_ij*e_j + G_ij*f_j)\n            #                 Q = f_i*(G_ij*e_j - B_ij*f_j) - e_i*(B_ij*e_j + G_ij*f_j)\n\n            P_calc += (e[i] * (G_ij * e[j] - B_ij * f[j]) +\n                      f[i] * (B_ij * e[j] + G_ij * f[j]))\n            Q_calc += (f[i] * (G_ij * e[j] - B_ij * f[j]) -\n                      e[i] * (B_ij * e[j] + G_ij * f[j]))\n\n        constraints.append(P_calc == pg_sum - pd)\n        constraints.append(Q_calc == qg_sum - qd)\n\n    # 5. Branch thermal limits (simplified - use loading factor)\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        # Calculate branch current (simplified)\n        # This is approximate - full AC branch equations are complex\n        z_mag = math.sqrt(r**2 + x**2)\n        current_limit = limit / 1.0  # Approximate\n\n        # Voltage drop across branch\n        V_f = e[f_bus_idx] + 1j * f[f_bus_idx]\n        V_t = e[t_bus_idx] + 1j * f[t_bus_idx]\n\n        # Current through branch (approximation)\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            V_f_adj = V_f / tap\n        else:\n            V_f_adj = V_f\n\n        I_f = (V_f_adj - V_t) / (r + 1j * x)\n\n        # Limit |I_f| <= current_limit\n        constraints.append(cp.abs(I_f) <= current_limit)\n\n    # 6. Voltage angle difference limits\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        # Angle difference: atan2(f, e) difference\n        # Approximate using: theta_i - theta_j ≈ (f_i*e_j - e_i*f_j)/(e_i*e_j + f_i*f_j)\n        numerator = f[f_bus_idx] * e[t_bus_idx] - e[f_bus_idx] * f[t_bus_idx]\n        denominator = e[f_bus_idx] * e[t_bus_idx] + f[f_bus_idx] * f[t_bus_idx]\n\n        # Avoid division by zero\n        epsilon = 1e-6\n        angle_diff = cp.atan(numerator / (denominator + epsilon))\n\n        constraints.append(angle_diff >= math.radians(br['angmin']))\n        constraints.append(angle_diff <= math.radians(br['angmax']))\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'model': 2, 'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n        cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n        if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n            c2, c1, c0 = cost_coeffs[-3], cost_coeffs[-2], cost_coeffs[-1]\n        else:\n            c2, c1, c0 = 0, 1, 0\n\n        total_cost += c2 * (pg[i] ** 2) + c1 * pg[i] + c0\n\n    objective = cp.Minimize(total_cost)\n\n    # Solve\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=True, max_iters=1000)\n\n        if problem.status in [\"infeasible\", \"unbounded\"]:\n            status = \"infeasible\"\n        else:\n            status = \"optimal\"\n\n        # Convert rectangular to polar\n        vm = np.sqrt(e.value**2 + f.value**2)\n        va = np.arctan2(f.value, e.value)\n\n        result = {\n            'status': status,\n            'vm': vm,\n            'va': va,\n            'e': e.value,\n            'f': f.value,\n            'pg': pg.value,\n            'qg': qg.value,\n            'objective_value': problem.objective.value\n        }\n\n        return result\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error solving AC-OPF: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return {\n            'status': 'error',\n            'error': str(e)\n        }\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    flows = []\n    e = solution['e']\n    f = solution['f']\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        # Calculate voltages\n        V_f = e[f_bus_idx] + 1j * f[f_bus_idx]\n        V_t = e[t_bus_idx] + 1j * f[t_bus_idx]\n\n        # Apply transformer\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            V_f_adj = V_f / tap\n        else:\n            V_f_adj = V_f\n\n        # Calculate power flow\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        I_f = (V_f_adj - V_t) / z\n        S_from = V_f_adj * np.conj(I_f)\n\n        # Reverse direction\n        I_t = (V_t - V_f_adj) / z\n        S_to = V_t * np.conj(I_t)\n\n        s_from_mva = np.abs(S_from)\n        s_to_mva = np.abs(S_to)\n\n        loading = max(s_from_mva, s_to_mva) / limit * 100 if limit > 0 else 0\n\n        flows.append({\n            'from_bus': br['f_bus'],\n            'to_bus': br['t_bus'],\n            'pf_from': np.real(S_from),\n            'qf_from': np.imag(S_from),\n            'pt_from': np.real(S_to),\n            'qt_from': np.imag(S_to),\n            's_from_mva': s_from_mva,\n            's_to_mva': s_to_mva,\n            'limit': limit,\n            'loading_pct': loading\n        })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef generate_report(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n\n    # Calculate summary statistics\n    total_pg = np.sum(solution['pg'])\n    total_qg = np.sum(solution['qg'])\n    total_pd = np.sum([b['pd'] for b in buses])\n    total_qd = np.sum([b['qd'] for b in buses])\n    total_losses = total_pg - total_pd\n\n    # Calculate feasibility metrics\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n Generator real and reactive power\n    pg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='pg')  # Real power\n    qg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='qg')  # Reactive power\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle constraint: angle = 0\n    constraints.append(va[ref_bus_idx] == 0)\n\n    # 2. vf * cp.conj(vt * y)\n                pf_from += cp.real(s_flow)\n                qf_from += cp.imag(s_flow)\n\n            elif t_bus_idx == i:\n                # This bus is the \"to\" bus\n                r = br['br_r']\n                x = br['br_x']\n                b = br['br_b']\n                tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n\n                y = 1.0 / (r + 1j * x)\n                y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n                f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n                t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n                vf = vm[f_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n                vt = vm[t_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n                if tap != 1.0:\n                    vf = vf / tap\n\n                # Complex power flow from t to f (reverse direction)\n                s_flow = vt * cp.conj(vt * (y + y_shunt)) - qd\n\n        # Power balance: net injection = power flowing out\n        constraints.append(net_p == pf_from + pf_to)\n        constraints.append(net_q == qf_from + qf_to)\n\n    # 5. Branch thermal limits\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        y = 1.0 / (r + 1j * x)\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        vf = vm[f_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n        vt = vm[t_bus_idx] * cp.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            vf = vf / tap\n\n        s_from = vf * cp.conj(vf * (y + y_shunt)) - vf * cp.conj(vt * y)\n        s_to = vt * cp.conj(vt * (y + y_shunt)) - vt * cp.conj(vf * y)\n\n        # Limit apparent power flow\n        constraints.append(cp.abs(s_from) <= limit)\n        constraints.append(cp.abs(s_to) <= limit)\n\n    # 6. Voltage angle difference limits\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = math.radians(br['angmax']))\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'model': 2, 'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n        cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n        if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n            c2, c1, c0 = cost_coeffs[-3], cost_coeffs[-2], cost_coeffs[-1]\n        else:\n            c2, c1, c0 = 0, 1, 0\n\n        # Cost is quadratic in MW (convert to $/hr)\n        total_cost += c2 * (pg[i] ** 2) + c1 * pg[i] + c0\n\n    objective = cp.Minimize(total_cost)\n\n    # Solve the problem\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=True)\n\n        if problem.status in [\"infeasible\", \"unbounded\"]:\n            status = \"infeasible\"\n        else:\n            status = \"optimal\"\n\n        # Extract results\n problem.objective.value\n        }\n\n        return result\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error solving AC-OPF: {e}\")\n        return {\n            'status': 'error',\n            'error': str(e)\n        }\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n Simplified flow limit - skip for now as it's complex in rectangular coords\n        # We'll calculate actual flows after solving\n        pass","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow (AC-OPF) Solver for ISO Base Case Creation\nUses the mathematical model from math-model.md to find least-cost, AC-feasible operating point\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport cvxpy as cp\nimport math\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load network data from JSON file in MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    base_mva = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    bus_fields = {\n        'bus_id': 0, 'bus_type': 1, 'pd': 2, 'qd': 3, 'gs': 4, 'bs': 5,\n        'area': 6, 'vm': 7, 'va': 8, 'base_kv': 9, 'zone': 10, 'vmax': 11, 'vmin': 12\n    }\n\n    buses = []\n    for b in data['bus']:\n        buses.append({\n            'bus_id': int(b[bus_fields['bus_id']]),\n            'bus_type': int(b[bus_fields['bus_type']]),\n            'pd': b[bus_fields['pd']],\n            'qd': b[bus_fields['qd']],\n            'gs': b[bus_fields['gs']],\n            'bs': b[bus_fields['bs']],\n            'area': b[bus_fields['area']],\n            'vm': b[bus_fields['vm']],\n            'va': b[bus_fields['va']],\n            'base_kv': b[bus_fields['base_kv']],\n            'zone': b[bus_fields['zone']],\n            'vmax': b[bus_fields['vmax']],\n            'vmin': b[bus_fields['vmin']]\n        })\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    gen_fields = {\n        'gen_bus': 0, 'pg': 1, 'qg': 2, 'qmax': 3, 'qmin': 4,\n        'vg': 5, 'mbase': 6, 'status': 7, 'pmax': 8, 'pmin': 9\n    }\n\n    generators = []\n    for g in data['gen']:\n        generators.append({\n            'gen_bus': int(g[gen_fields['gen_bus']]),\n            'pg': g[gen_fields['pg']],\n            'qg': g[gen_fields['qg']],\n            'qmax': g[gen_fields['qmax']],\n            'qmin': g[gen_fields['qmin']],\n            'vg': g[gen_fields['vg']],\n            'mbase': g[gen_fields['mbase']],\n            'status': int(g[gen_fields['status']]),\n            'pmax': g[gen_fields['pmax']],\n            'pmin': g[gen_fields['pmin']]\n        })\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branch_fields = {\n        'f_bus': 0, 't_bus': 1, 'br_r': 2, 'br_x': 3, 'br_b': 4,\n        'rate_a': 5, 'rate_b': 6, 'rate_c': 7, 'ratio': 8, 'angle': 9,\n        'status': 10, 'angmin': 11, 'angmax': 12\n    }\n\n    branches = []\n    for br in data['branch']:\n        branches.append({\n            'f_bus': int(br[branch_fields['f_bus']]),\n            't_bus': int(br[branch_fields['t_bus']]),\n            'br_r': br[branch_fields['br_r']],\n            'br_x': br[branch_fields['br_x']],\n            'br_b': br[branch_fields['br_b']],\n            'rate_a': br[branch_fields['rate_a']],\n            'ratio': br[branch_fields['ratio']],\n            'angle': br[branch_fields['angle']],\n            'status': int(br[branch_fields['status']]),\n            'angmin': br[branch_fields['angmin']],\n            'angmax': br[branch_fields['angmax']]\n        })\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    gencost = []\n    for gc in data['gencost']:\n        model = int(gc[0])\n        startup = gc[1]\n        shutdown = gc[2]\n        ncost = int(gc[3])\n        cost_coeffs = gc[4:]\n        gencost.append({\n            'model': model,\n            'startup': startup,\n            'shutdown': shutdown,\n            'ncost': ncost,\n            'cost': cost_coeffs\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'base_mva': base_mva,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n        'gencost': gencost\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_data: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC-OPF using rectangular coordinates formulation\n    \"\"\"\n    base_mva = network_data['base_mva']\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    gencost = network_data['gencost']\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Create mappings\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    # Identify reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n        if b['bus_type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Variables in rectangular coordinates\n    # V = e + j*f where e = real part, f = imaginary part\n    e = cp.Variable(n_buses, name='e')  # Real part of voltage\n    f = cp.Variable(n_buses, name='f')  # Imaginary part of voltage\n\n    # Generator real and reactive power\n    pg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='pg')  # Real power\n    qg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='qg')  # Reactive power\n\n    # Helper to calculate voltage magnitude squared\n    def vm_sq(i):\n        return e[i]**2 + f[i]**2\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus constraints\n    constraints.append(e[ref_bus_idx] >= 0.1)  # e > 0\n    constraints.append(f[ref_bus_idx] == 0)    # f = 0 at reference bus\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] == 1:\n            constraints.append(pg[i] >= gen['pmin'])\n            constraints.append(pg[i] <= gen['pmax'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] >= gen['qmin'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] <= gen['qmax'])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(pg[i] == 0)\n            constraints.append(qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds (convert to e^2 + f^2 bounds)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        vmin_sq = bus['vmin']**2\n        vmax_sq = bus['vmax']**2\n        constraints.append(vm_sq(i) >= vmin_sq)\n        constraints.append(vm_sq(i) <= vmax_sq)\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch admittances\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        angle_shift = br['angle'] * math.pi / 180\n\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Apply transformer if not unity tap\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            a = tap * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt) / (abs(a)**2)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y / np.conj(a)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y / a\n        else:\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y\n\n    # Add shunts\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['gs'] != 0 or bus['bs'] != 0:\n            Y[i, i] += (bus['gs'] + 1j * bus['bs'])\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        # Generators connected to this bus\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        pg_sum = cp.sum([pg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = cp.sum([qg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        pd = bus['pd']\n        qd = bus['qd']\n\n        # Complex power injection\n        S_inj = 0 + 0j\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            V_i = e[i] + 1j * f[i]\n            V_j = e[j] + 1j * f[j]\n            S_inj += cp.conj(Y[i, j]) * V_i * (cp.conj(V_i) - cp.conj(V_j))\n\n        # Actually, let's use the standard power flow equations\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            G_ij = cp.real(Y[i, j])\n            B_ij = cp.imag(Y[i, j])\n\n            # P_i = sum(V_i*V_j*(G_ij*cos(theta_i - theta_j) + B_ij*sin(theta_i - theta_j)))\n            # Q_i = sum(V_i*V_j*(G_ij*sin(theta_i - theta_j) - B_ij*cos(theta_i - theta_j)))\n\n            # Using rectangular: P = e_i*(G_ij*e_j - B_ij*f_j) + f_i*(B_ij*e_j + G_ij*f_j)\n            #                 Q = f_i*(G_ij*e_j - B_ij*f_j) - e_i*(B_ij*e_j + G_ij*f_j)\n\n            P_calc += (e[i] * (G_ij * e[j] - B_ij * f[j]) +\n                      f[i] * (B_ij * e[j] + G_ij * f[j]))\n            Q_calc += (f[i] * (G_ij * e[j] - B_ij * f[j]) -\n                      e[i] * (B_ij * e[j] + G_ij * f[j]))\n\n        constraints.append(P_calc == pg_sum - pd)\n        constraints.append(Q_calc == qg_sum - qd)\n\n    # 5. Branch thermal limits (simplified - use loading factor)\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        # Calculate branch current (simplified)\n        # This is approximate - full AC branch equations are complex\n        z_mag = math.sqrt(r**2 + x**2)\n        current_limit = limit / 1.0  # Approximate\n\n        # Voltage drop across branch\n        V_f = e[f_bus_idx] + 1j * f[f_bus_idx]\n        V_t = e[t_bus_idx] + 1j * f[t_bus_idx]\n\n        # Current through branch (approximation)\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            V_f_adj = V_f / tap\n        else:\n            V_f_adj = V_f\n\n        I_f = (V_f_adj - V_t) / (r + 1j * x)\n\n        # Limit |I_f| <= current_limit\n        constraints.append(cp.abs(I_f) <= current_limit)\n\n    # 6. f[f_bus_idx] * e[t_bus_idx] - e[f_bus_idx] * f[t_bus_idx]\n        denominator = e[f_bus_idx] * e[t_bus_idx] + f[f_bus_idx] * f[t_bus_idx]\n\n        # Avoid division by zero\n        epsilon = 1e-6\n continue\n\n        cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'model': 2, 'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n        cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n        if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n            c2, c1, cp.Minimize(total_cost)\n\n    # Solve\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=True, max_iters=1000)\n\n        if matrix\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    Y = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        angle_shift = br['angle'] * math.pi / 180\n\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            a = tap * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt) / (abs(a)**2)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y / np.conj(a)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y / a\n        else:\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y\n\n    # Add shunts\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['gs'] != 0 or bus['bs'] != 0:\n            Y[i, i] += (bus['gs'] + 1j * bus['bs'])\n\n    return Y\n\ndef solve_acopf_scipy(network_data: Dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC-OPF using scipy.optimize\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    gencost = network_data['gencost']\n    base_mva = network_data['base_mva']\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Create mappings\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    # Identify reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n        if b['bus_type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = var_idx[f'va_{i}'] = idx\n        idx += 1\n    for i in range(n_gens):\n        var_idx[f'pg_{i}'] = idx\n        idx += 1\n    for i in range(n_gens):\n        var_idx[f'qg_{i}'] = idx\n        idx += 1\n\n    n_vars = idx\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n\n    # Initialize voltages\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        x0[var_idx[f'vm_{i}']] = bus['vm'] if bus['vm'] > 0 else 1.0\n        if i != ref_bus_idx:\n            x0[var_idx[f'va_{i}']] = bus['va'] * math.pi / 180\n\n    # Initialize generator outputs\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        x0[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']] = gen['pg']\n        x0[var_idx[f'qg_{i}']] = gen['qg']\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        bounds.append((bus['vmin'], bus['vmax']))\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds (only for non-reference buses)\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        if i == ref_bus_idx:\n            continue\n        bounds.append((-math.pi, math.pi))\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bounds.append((gen['pmin'], gen['pmax']))  # pg\n    for i, gen in mismatches for all buses\"\"\"\n        mismatches = []\n\n        # Extract variables\n        vm = np.array([x[var_idx[f'vm_{i}']] for i in range(n_buses)])\n        va = np.array([0.0 if i == ref_bus_idx else x[var_idx[f'va_{i}']] for i in range(n_buses)])\n\n        # Generator power at each bus\n        pg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        qg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen['gen_bus']]\n            pg_at_bus[bus_idx] = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n            qg_at_bus[bus_idx] = x[var_idx[f'qg_{i}']]\n\n        # Calculate power injections\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            P_calc = 0.0\n            Q_calc = 0.0\n\n            for j in range(n_buses):\n                G_ij = Y[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Y[i, j].imag\n\n                # Power flow equations\n                theta_diff = va[i] - va[j]\n                P_calc += vm[i] * vm[j] * (G_ij * math.cos(theta_diff) + B_ij * math.sin(theta_diff))\n                Q_calc += vm[i] * vm[j] * (G_ij * math.sin(theta_diff) - B_ij * math.cos(theta_diff))\n\n            P_mismatch = P_calc - (pg_at_bus[i] - buses[i]['pd'])\n            Q_mismatch = Q_calc - (qg_at_bus[i] - buses[i]['qd'])\n\n            mismatches.append(P_mismatch)\n            mismatches.append(Q_mismatch)\n\n        return np.array(mismatches)\n\n    # Inequality constraints: branch flow limits\n    def branch_limits(x):\n        \"\"\"Calculate branch flow violations\"\"\"\n        violations = []\n\n        vm = np.array([x[var_idx[f'vm_{i}']] for      continue\n\n            f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n            t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n            r = br['br_r']\n            x_val = br['br_x']\n            b = br['br_b']\n            tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n            limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n            # Calculate voltages\n            V_f = vm[f_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n            V_t = vm[t_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n            # Apply transformer\n            if tap != 1.0:\n                V_f = V_f / tap\n\n            # Calculate power flow\n            z = r + 1j * x_val\n            I_f = (V_f - V_t) / z\n            S_from = V_f * np.conj(I_f)\n\n            # Limit: |S| <= limit\n            violations.append(limit - np.abs(S_from))\n\n        return np.array(violations)\n\n    # Objective function\n    def objective(x):\n        \"\"\"Calculate total generation cost\"\"\"\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            if gen['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'model': 2, 'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n            cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n            if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n                c2, c1, c0 = cost_coeffs[-3], cost_coeffs[-2], cost_coeffs[-1]\n            else:\n                c2, c1, c0 = 0, 1, 0\n\n            pg = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n            total_cost += c2 * (pg ** 2) + c1 * pg + c0\n\n        return total_cost\n\n    # Set up constraints\n    eq_cons = {'type': 'eq', 'fun': power_balance}\n\n    # Branch limits as inequality constraints\n    ineq_cons = {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': branch_limits}\n\n    print(f\"Solving AC-OPF with {n_vars} variables...\")\n\n    # Solve\n    result = minimize(\n        objective,\n        x0,\n        method='SLSQP',\n        bounds=bounds,\n        constraints=[eq_cons, ineq_cons],\n        options={'maxiter': 1000, 'ftol': 1e-6, 'disp': True}\n    )\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Warning: Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n\n    # Extract solution\n    solution = {\n        'status': 'optimal' if result.success else 'infeasible',\n        'vm': np.array([result.x[var_idx[f'vm_{i}']] for i in range(n_buses)]),\n        'va': np.array([0.0 if i == ref_bus_idx else result.x[var_idx[f'va_{i}']] for i in range(n_buses)]),\n        'pg': np.array([result.x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']] for i in range(n_gens)]),\n        'qg': np.array([result.x[var_idx[f'qg_{i}']] for i power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    flows = []\n    vm = solution['vm']\n    va = solution['va']\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        # Calculate voltages\n        V_f = vm[f_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n        V_t = vm[t_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n        # Apply transformer\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            V_f = V_f / tap\n\n        # Calculate power flow\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        I_f = (V_f - V_t) / z\n        S_from = V_f * np.conj(I_f)\n\n        # Reverse direction\n        I_t = (V_t - V_f) / z\n        S_to = V_t * np.conj(I_t)\n\n        s_from_mva = np.abs(S_from)\n        s_to_mva = np.abs(S_to)\n\n        loading = max(s_from_mva, s_to_mva) / limit * 100 if limit > 0 else 0\n\n        flows.append({\n            'from_bus': br['f_bus'],\n            'to_bus': br['t_bus'],\n            'pf_from': np.real(S_from),\n            'qf_from': np.imag(S_from),\n            'pt_from': np.real(S_to),\n            'qt_from': np.imag(S_to),\n            's_from_mva': s_from_mva,\n            's_to_mva': s_to_mva,\n            'limit': limit,\n            'loading_pct': loading\n        })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef generate_report(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n\n    # Calculate summary statistics\n    total_pg = np.sum(solution['pg'])\n    total_qg = np.sum(solution['qg'])\n    total_pd = np.sum([b['pd'] for b in buses])\n    total_qd = np.sum([b['qd'] for b in buses])\n    total_losses = total_pg - total_pd\n\n    # Calculate feasibility metrics\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0.0\n    max_branch_overload = 0.0\n\n    # Check power balance at each bus\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        pg_sum = np.sum([solution['pg'][idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = np.sum([solution['qg'][idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        mismatch_p = abs(pg_sum - bus['pd'])\n        mismatch_q = abs(qg_sum - bus['qd'])\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, mismatch_p)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, mismatch_q)\n\n        # Check voltage violations\n        vm = solution['vm'][i]\n        if vm > bus['vmax']:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, vm - bus['vmax'])\n        elif vm < bus['vmin']:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, bus['vmin'] - vm)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    flows = calculate_branch_flows(network_data, solution)\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    for flow in flows:\n        if flow['s_from_mva'] > flow['limit']:\n            overload = flow['s_from_mva'] - flow['limit']\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Get most loaded branches\n    most_loaded = sorted(flows, key=lambda x: bus data\n    bus_data = []\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        bus_data.append({\n            'id': bus['bus_id'],\n            'vm_pu': float(solution['vm'][i]),\n            'va_deg': float(np.degrees(solution['va'][i])),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax'])\n        })\n\n    # Format most loaded branches\n    most_loaded_branches = []\n    for flow in most_loaded:\n        most_loaded_branches.append({\n            'from_bus': flow['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': flow['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct':
