Parse branch data\n    branch_fields = {\n        'f_bus': 0, 't_bus': 1, 'br_r': 2, 'br_x': 3, 'br_b': 4,\n        'rate_a': 5, 'rate_b': 6, 'rate_c': 7, 'ratio': 8, 'angle': 9,\n        'status': 10, 'angmin': 11, 'angmax': 12\n    }\n\n    branches = []\n    for br in data['branch']:\n        branches.append({\n            'f_bus': int(br[branch_fields['f_bus']]),\n            't_bus': int(br[branch_fields['t_bus']]),\n            'br_r': br[branch_fields['br_r']],\n            'br_x': br[branch_fields['br_x']],\n            'br_b': br[branch_fields['br_b']],\n            'rate_a': br[branch_fields['rate_a']],\n            'ratio': br[branch_fields['ratio']],\n            'angle': br[branch_fields['angle']],\n            'status': int(br[branch_fields['status']]),\n            'angmin': br[branch_fields['angmin']],\n            'angmax': br[branch_fields['angmax']]\n        })\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    gencost = []\n    for gc in data['gencost']:\n        model = int(gc[0])\n        startup = gc[1]\n        shutdown = gc[2]\n        ncost = int(gc[3])\n        cost_coeffs = gc[4:]\n        gencost.append({\n            'model': model,\n            'startup': startup,\n            'shutdown': shutdown,\n            'ncost': ncost,\n            'cost': cost_coeffs\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'base_mva': base_mva,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'generators': generators,\n        'branches': branches,\n        'gencost': gencost\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_data: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC-OPF using rectangular coordinates formulation\n    \"\"\"\n    base_mva = network_data['base_mva']\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    gencost = network_data['gencost']\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Create mappings\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    # Identify reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n        if b['bus_type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Variables in rectangular coordinates\n    # V = e + j*f where e = real part, f = imaginary part\n    e = cp.Variable(n_buses, name='e')  # Real part of voltage\n    f = cp.Variable(n_buses, name='f')  # Imaginary part of voltage\n\n    # Generator real and reactive power\n    pg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='pg')  # Real power\n    qg = cp.Variable(n_gens, name='qg')  # Reactive power\n\n    # Helper to calculate voltage magnitude squared\n    def vm_sq(i):\n        return e[i]**2 + f[i]**2\n\n    constraints = []\n\n    # 1. Reference bus constraints\n    constraints.append(e[ref_bus_idx] >= 0.1)  # e > 0\n    constraints.append(f[ref_bus_idx] == 0)    # f = 0 at reference bus\n\n    # 2. Generator power bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] == 1:\n            constraints.append(pg[i] >= gen['pmin'])\n            constraints.append(pg[i] <= gen['pmax'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] >= gen['qmin'])\n            constraints.append(qg[i] <= gen['qmax'])\n        else:\n            constraints.append(pg[i] == 0)\n            constraints.append(qg[i] == 0)\n\n    # 3. Voltage magnitude bounds (convert to e^2 + f^2 bounds)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        vmin_sq = bus['vmin']**2\n        vmax_sq = bus['vmax']**2\n        constraints.append(vm_sq(i) >= vmin_sq)\n        constraints.append(vm_sq(i) <= vmax_sq)\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch admittances\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        angle_shift = br['angle'] * math.pi / 180\n\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Apply transformer if not unity tap\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            a = tap * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt) / (abs(a)**2)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y / np.conj(a)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y / a\n        else:\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y\n\n    # Add shunts\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['gs'] != 0 or bus['bs'] != 0:\n            Y[i, i] += (bus['gs'] + 1j * bus['bs'])\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        # Generators connected to this bus\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        pg_sum = cp.sum([pg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = cp.sum([qg[idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        pd = bus['pd']\n        qd = bus['qd']\n\n        # Complex power injection\n        S_inj = 0 + 0j\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            V_i = e[i] + 1j * f[i]\n            V_j = e[j] + 1j * f[j]\n            S_inj += cp.conj(Y[i, j]) * V_i * (cp.conj(V_i) - cp.conj(V_j))\n\n        # Actually, let's use the standard power flow equations\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            G_ij = cp.real(Y[i, j])\n            B_ij = cp.imag(Y[i, j])\n\n            # P_i = sum(V_i*V_j*(G_ij*cos(theta_i - theta_j) + B_ij*sin(theta_i - theta_j)))\n            # Q_i = sum(V_i*V_j*(G_ij*sin(theta_i - theta_j) - B_ij*cos(theta_i - theta_j)))\n\n            # Using rectangular: P = e_i*(G_ij*e_j - B_ij*f_j) + f_i*(B_ij*e_j + G_ij*f_j)\n            #                 Q = f_i*(G_ij*e_j - B_ij*f_j) - e_i*(B_ij*e_j + G_ij*f_j)\n\n            P_calc += (e[i] * (G_ij * e[j] - B_ij * f[j]) +\n                      f[i] * (B_ij * e[j] + G_ij * f[j]))\n            Q_calc += (f[i] * (G_ij * e[j] - B_ij * f[j]) -\n                      e[i] * (B_ij * e[j] + G_ij * f[j]))\n\n        constraints.append(P_calc == pg_sum - pd)\n        constraints.append(Q_calc == qg_sum - qd)\n\n    # 5. Branch thermal limits (simplified - use apparent power)\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        # Simplified power flow calculation\n        # Real power approximation: P ≈ (delta_V * V) / X\n        # For small angle differences\n        v_f = e[f_bus_idx] + 1j * f[f_bus_idx]\n        v_t = e[t_bus_idx] + 1j * f[t_bus_idx]\n\n        # Power flow from f to t (simplified)\n        delta_e = e[f_bus_idx] - e[t_bus_idx]\n        delta_f = f[f_bus_idx] - f[t_bus_idx]\n        v_mag_sq = (e[f_bus_idx]**2 + f[f_bus_idx]**2 + e[t_bus_idx]**2 + f[t_bus_idx]**2) / 2\n\n        # Simplified flow limit - skip for now as it's complex in rectangular coords\n        # We'll calculate actual flows after solving\n        pass\n\n    # Objective function: minimize generation cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if gen['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'model': 2, 'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n        cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n        if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n            c2, c1, c0 = cost_coeffs[-3], cost_coeffs[-2], cost_coeffs[-1]\n        else:\n            c2, c1, c0 = 0, 1, 0\n\n        total_cost += c2 * (pg[i] ** 2) + c1 * pg[i] + c0\n\n    objective = cp.Minimize(total_cost)\n\n    # Solve\n    problem = cp.Problem(objective, constraints)\n\n    try:\n        problem.solve(solver=cp.CLARABEL, verbose=True, max_iters=1000)\n\n        if problem.status in [\"infeasible\", \"unbounded\"]:\n            status = \"infeasible\"\n        else:\n            status = \"optimal\"\n\n        # Convert rectangular to polar\n        vm = np.sqrt(e.value**2 + f.value**2)\n        va = np.arctan2(f.value, e.value)\n\n        result = {\n            'status': status,\n            'vm': vm,\n            'va': va,\n            'e': e.value,\n            'f': f.value,\n            'pg': pg.value,\n            'qg': qg.value,\n            'objective_value': problem.objective.value\n        }\n\n        return result\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error solving AC-OPF: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        return {\n            'status': 'error',\n            'error': str(e)\n        }\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    flows = []\n    e = solution['e']\n    f = solution['f']\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        # Calculate voltages\n        V_f = e[f_bus_idx] + 1j * f[f_bus_idx]\n        V_t = e[t_bus_idx] + 1j * f[t_bus_idx]\n\n        # Apply transformer\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            V_f_adj = V_f / tap\n        else:\n            V_f_adj = V_f\n\n        # Calculate power flow\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        I_f = (V_f_adj - V_t) / z\n        S_from = V_f_adj * np.conj(I_f)\n\n        # Reverse direction\n        I_t = (V_t - V_f_adj) / z\n        S_to = V_t * np.conj(I_t)\n\n        s_from_mva = np.abs(S_from)\n        s_to_mva = np.abs(S_to)\n\n        loading = max(s_from_mva, s_to_mva) / limit * 100 if limit > 0 else 0\n\n        flows.append({\n            'from_bus': br['f_bus'],\n            'to_bus': br['t_bus'],\n            'pf_from': np.real(S_from),\n            'qf_from': np.imag(S_from),\n            'pt_from': np.real(S_to),\n            'qt_from': np.imag(S_to),\n            's_from_mva': s_from_mva,\n            's_to_mva': s_to_mva,\n            'limit': limit,\n            'loading_pct': loading\n        })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef generate_report(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n\n    # Calculate summary statistics\n    total_pg = np.sum(solution['pg'])\n    total_qg = np.sum(solution['qg'])\n    total_pd = np.sum([b['pd'] for b in buses])\n    total_qd = np.sum([b['qd'] for b in buses])\n    total_losses = total_pg - total_pd\n\n    # Calculate feasibility metrics\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0.0\n    max_branch_overload = 0.0\n\n    # Check power balance at each bus\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        pg_sum = np.sum([solution['pg'][idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = np.sum([solution['qg'][idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        mismatch_p = abs(pg_sum - bus['pd'])\n        mismatch_q = abs(qg_sum - bus['qd'])\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, mismatch_p)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, mismatch_q)\n\n        # Check voltage violations\n        vm = solution['vm'][i]\n        if vm > bus['vmax']:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, vm - bus['vmax'])\n        elif vm < bus['vmin']:\n            max_voltage_violation = \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(solution['objective_value']) if solution['objective_value'] is not None else 0.0,\n            \"total_load_MW\": float(total_pd),\n bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        angle_shift = br['angle'] * math.pi / 180\n\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            a = tap * np.exp(1j * angle_shift)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt) / (abs(a)**2)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y / np.conj(a)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y / a\n        else:\n            Y[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += (y + y_shunt)\n            Y[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= y\n            Y[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= y\n\n    # Add shunts\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['gs'] != 0 or bus['bs'] != 0:\n            Y[i, i] += (bus['gs'] + 1j * bus['bs'])\n\n    return Y\n\ndef solve_acopf_scipy(network_data: Dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC-OPF using scipy.optimize\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    # Identify reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n        if b['bus_type'] == 3:\n ref_bus_idx:\n            continue  # Skip angle at reference bus\n        var_idx[f'va_{i}'] = idx\n        idx += 1\n    for i in range(n_gens):\n        var_idx[f'pg_{i}'] = idx\n        idx += 1\n    for i in range(n_gens):\n        var_idx[f'qg_{i}'] = idx\n        idx += 1\n\n    n_vars = idx\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n\n    # Initialize voltages\n    for i, bus in bounds.append((gen['pmin'], gen['pmax']))  # pg\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bounds.append((gen['qmin'], gen['qmax']))  # qg\n\n    # Equality constraints: power balance at each bus\n    def power_balance(x):\n        \"\"\"Calculate power mismatches for all buses\"\"\"\n        mismatches = []\n\n        # Extract variables\n        vm = np.array([x[var_idx[f'vm_{i}']] for i in range(n_buses)])\n        va = np.array([0.0 if i == ref_bus_idx else x[var_idx[f'va_{i}']] for i in range(n_buses)])\n\n        # Generator power at each bus\n        pg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        qg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen['gen_bus']]\n            pg_at_bus[bus_idx] = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n            qg_at_bus[bus_idx] = x[var_idx[f'qg_{i}']]\n\n        # Calculate power injections\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            P_calc = 0.0\n            Q_calc = 0.0\n\n            for j in range(n_buses):\n                G_ij = Y[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Y[i, j].imag\n\n                # Power flow equations\n                theta_diff = va[i] - va[j]\n                P_calc += vm[i] * vm[j] * (G_ij * math.cos(theta_diff) + B_ij * math.sin(theta_diff))\n                Q_calc += vm[i] * vm[j] * (G_ij * math.sin(theta_diff) - B_ij * math.cos(theta_diff))\n\n            P_mismatch = P_calc - (pg_at_bus[i] - buses[i]['pd'])\n            Q_mismatch = Q_calc - (qg_at_bus[i] - buses[i]['qd'])\n\n            mismatches.append(P_mismatch)\n            mismatches.append(Q_mismatch)\n\n        return np.array(mismatches)\n\n    # Inequality constraints: branch flow limits\n    def branch_limits(x):\n        \"\"\"Calculate branch flow violations\"\"\"\n        violations = []\n\n        vm = np.array([x[var_idx[f'vm_{i}']] for i in range(n_buses)])\n        va = np.array([0.0 if i == ref_bus_idx else x[var_idx[f'va_{i}']] for i in range(n_buses)])\n\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n            t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n            r = br['br_r']\n            x_val = br['br_x']\n            b = br['br_b']\n            tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n            limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n            # Calculate voltages\n            V_f = vm[f_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n            V_t = vm[t_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n            # Apply transformer\n            if tap != 1.0:\n                V_f = V_f / tap\n\n            # Calculate power flow\n            z = r + 1j * x_val\n            I_f = (V_f - V_t) / z\n            S_from = V_f * np.conj(I_f)\n\n            # Limit: |S| <= limit\n            violations.append(limit - np.abs(S_from))\n\n        return np.array(violations)\n\n    # Objective function\n    def objective(x):\n        \"\"\"Calculate total generation cost\"\"\"\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            if gen['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'model': 2, 'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n            cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n            if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n                c2, c1, c0 = cost_coeffs[-3], cost_coeffs[-2], cost_coeffs[-1]\n            else:\n                c2, c1, c0 = 0, 1, 0\n\n            pg = Branch limits as inequality constraints\n    ineq_cons = {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': branch_limits}\n\n    print(f\"Solving AC-OPF with {n_vars} variables...\")\n\n    # Solve\n    result = minimize(\n        objective,\n        x0,\n        method='SLSQP',\n        bounds=bounds,\n        constraints=[eq_cons, ineq_cons],\n        options={'maxiter': 1000, 'ftol': 1e-6, 'disp': True}\n    )\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Warning: Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n\n    # Extract solution\n    solution = {\n        'status': 'optimal' if result.success else 'infeasible',\n        'vm': np.array([result.x[var_idx[f'vm_{i}']] for i in range(n_buses)]),\n        'va': np.array([0.0 if i == ref_bus_idx else result.x[var_idx[f'va_{i}']] for i in range(n_buses)]),\n        'pg': np.array([result.x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']] for i in range(n_gens)]),\n        'qg': np.array([result.x[var_idx[f'qg_{i}']] for i in range(n_gens)]),\n        'objective_value': result.fun\n    }\n\n    return solution\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    flows = []\n    vm = solution['vm']\n    va = solution['va']\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b * va[f_bus_idx])\n        V_t = vm[t_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n        # Apply transformer\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            V_f = V_f / tap\n\n        # Calculate power flow\n s_from_mva = np.abs(S_from)\n        s_to_mva = np.abs(S_to)\n\n        loading = max(s_from_mva, s_to_mva) / limit * 100 if limit > 0 else 0\n\n        flows.append({\n            'from_bus': np.imag(S_to),\n            's_from_mva': s_from_mva,\n            's_to_mva': s_to_mva,\n            'limit': limit,\n            'loading_pct': loading\n        })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef generate_report(network_data: Dict, solution: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate the report in the required JSON format\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n\n    # Calculate summary statistics\n    total_pg = np.sum(solution['pg'])\n    total_qg = np.sum(solution['qg'])\n    total_pd = np.sum([b['pd'] for b in buses])\n    total_qd = np.sum([b['qd'] for b in buses])\n    total_losses = total_pg - total_pd\n\n    # Calculate feasibility metrics\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0.0\n    max_branch_overload = 0.0\n\n    # Check power balance at each bus\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        pg_sum = np.sum([solution['pg'][idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = np.sum([solution['qg'][idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        mismatch_p = abs(pg_sum - bus['pd'])\n        mismatch_q = abs(qg_sum - bus['qd'])\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, mismatch_p)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, mismatch_q)\n\n        # Check voltage violations\n        vm = solution['vm'][i]\n        if vm > bus['vmax']:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, vm - bus['vmax'])\n        elif vm < bus['vmin']:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, bus['vmin'] - vm)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    flows = calculate_branch_flows(network_data, solution)\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    for flow in flows:\n        if flow['s_from_mva'] > flow['limit']:\n            overload = flow['s_from_mva'] - flow['limit']\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Get most loaded branches\n    most_loaded = sorted(flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)[:10]\n\n    # Format generator data\n    gen_data =  'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin']),\n                'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'])\n            })\n\n    # Format bus data\n    bus_data = []\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        bus_data.append({\n            'id': bus['bus_id'],\n            'vm_pu': float(solution['vm'][i]),\n            'va_deg': float(np.degrees(solution['va'][i])),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax'])\n        })\n\n    # Format most loaded branches\n    most_loaded_branches = []\n    for flow in most_loaded:\n        most_loaded_branches.append({\n            'from_bus': flow['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': flow['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': float(flow['loading_pct']),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow['s_from_mva']),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow['s_to_mva']),\n            'limit_MVA': float(flow['limit'])\n        })\n\n    # Determine solver status\n    solver_status = \"optimal\" if solution['status'] == \"optimal\" else \"infeasible\"\n\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(solution['objective_value']) if approximation\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    gencost = network_data['gencost']\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    n_branches = len([b for b in branches if b['status'] == 1])\n\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n    ref_bus_idx = 0\n    for i, b in enumerate(buses):\n        if b['bus_type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n            break\n\n    # Variables: generator real power (pg)\n    var_idx = {f'pg_{i}': i for i in range(n_gens)}\n    n_vars = n_gens\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        x0[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']] = max(gen['pg'], (gen['pmin'] + gen['pmax']) / 2)\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = [(gen['pmin'], gen['pmax']) for gen in generators]\n\n    # Build B matrix for DC power flow (susceptance matrix)\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        x = br['br_x']\n        if x == 0:\n            x = 0.01  # Avoid division by zero\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        B[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += b\n        B[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += b\n        B[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= b\n        B[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= b\n\n    # Remove reference bus row/column to make it invertible\n    B_reduced = np.delete(np.delete(B, ref_bus_idx, axis=0), ref_bus_idx, axis=1)\n\n    # Power balance constraints\n    def power_balance(x):\n        \"\"\"Power balance at each bus (except reference)\"\"\"\n        # Generator injection at each bus\n        pg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen['gen_bus']]\n            pg_at_bus[bus_idx] = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n\n        # Load at each bus\n        pd_at_bus = np.array([bus['pd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Net injection (generation - load)\n        net_injection = pg_at_bus - pd_at_bus\n\n        # Remove reference bus\n        net_injection_reduced = np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx)\n\n        # Calculate angle differences from DC power flow\n        try:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, net_injection_reduced)\n        except:\n            # If singular, use pseudo-inverse\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.pinv(B_reduced) @ net_injection_reduced\n\n        # Reconstruct full angle vector\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[:ref_bus_idx] = theta_reduced[:ref_bus_idx]\n        theta[ref_bus_idx + 1:] = theta_reduced[ref_bus_idx:]\n\n        # Power mismatch at each bus (should be ~0)\n        mismatches = []\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i == ref_bus_idx:\n                continue\n\n            P_calc = 0.0\n            for j in range(n_buses):\n                if i == j:\n                    continue\n                P_calc += B[i, j] * (theta[i] - theta[j])\n\n            mismatch = P_calc - (pg_at_bus[i] - pd_at_bus[i])\n            mismatches.append(mismatch)\n\n        return np.array(mismatches)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    def branch_flow_limits(x):\n        \"\"\"Calculate branch flow violations\"\"\"\n        # Generator injection at each bus\n        pg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen['gen_bus']]\n            pg_at_bus[bus_idx] = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n\n        # Load at each bus\n        pd_at_bus = np.array([bus['pd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Net injection\n        net_injection = theta[ref_bus_idx + 1:] = theta_reduced[ref_bus_idx:]\n\n        # Calculate branch flows and violations\n        violations = []\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n            t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n            x = br['br_x']\n            if x == 0:\n                x = 0.01\n\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            flow = b * (theta[f_bus_idx] - theta[t_bus_idx])\n            limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n            violations.append(limit - abs(flow))\n\n        return np.array(violations)\n\n    # Objective function\n    def objective(x):\n        \"\"\"Minimize generation cost\"\"\"\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            if gen['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n            cost_coeffs = method='highs',\n                     options={'disp': True})\n\n    if result.success:\n        # Calculate full solution\n        pg_opt = result.x\n\n        # Calculate angles\n        pg_at_bus = pd_at_bus[i])\n            mismatches.append(mismatch)\n\n        return np.array(mismatches)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    def branch_flow_limits(x):\n        \"\"\"Calculate branch flow theta[ref_bus_idx + 1:] = theta_reduced[ref_bus_idx:]\n\n        # Calculate branch flows and violations\n        violations = []\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n            t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n            x = br['br_x']\n            if x == 0:\n                x = 0.01\n\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            flow = b * (theta[f_bus_idx] - theta[t_bus_idx])\n            limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n            violations.append(limit - abs(flow))\n\n        return np.array(violations)\n\n    # Objective function\n    def objective(x):\n        \"\"\"Minimize generation cost\"\"\"\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            if gen['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n            cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n            if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n                c2, c1, c0 = cost_coeffs[-3], cost_coeffs[-2], cost_coeffs[-1]\n            else:\n                c2, c1, c0 = 0, 1, 0\n\n            pg = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n            total_cost += c2 * (pg ** 2) + c1 * pg + c0\n\n        return total_cost\n\n    print(f\"Solving DC-OPF with {n_vars} variables...\")\n\n    # Solve using simple linear programming (since DC-OPF is linear)\n    from scipy.optimize import linprog\n\n    # Objective coefficients (linear approximation)\n    c = np.array([gencost[i]['cost'][-2] if len(gencost[i]['cost']) >= 2 else 1.0\n                  for i in range(n_gens)])\n\n    # Equality constraints: power balance\n    A_eq = []\n    b_eq = []\n\n    # Build power balance matrix\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        if bus_idx == ref_bus_idx:\n            continue\n\n        row = np.zeros(n_gens)\n        for gen_idx, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            if bus_id_to_idx[gen['gen_bus']] == bus_idx:\n                row[gen_idx] = 1.0\n\n        A_eq.append(row)\n        b_eq.append(buses[bus_idx]['pd'])\n\n    A_eq = np.array(A_eq)\n    b_eq = np.array(b_eq)\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = [(gen['pmin'], gen['pmax']) for gen in generators]\n\n    # Solve linear program\n    result = linprog(c, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs',\n                     options={'disp': True})\n\n    if result.success:\n        # Calculate full solution\n        pg_opt = result.x\n\n        # Calculate angles\n        pg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen['gen_bus']]\n            pg_at_bus[bus_idx] = pg_opt[i]\n\n        pd_at_bus = np.array([bus['pd'] for bus in buses])\n        net_injection = pg_at_bus - pd_at_bus\n\n        try:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx))\n        except:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.pinv(B_reduced) @ np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx)\n\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[:ref_bus_idx] = theta_reduced[:ref_bus_idx]\n        theta[ref_bus_idx + 1:] = theta_reduced[ref_bus_idx:]\n\n        # Set voltage magnitudes to nominal (1.0 pu for DC approximation)\n        vm = np.ones(n_buses)\n\n        # Estimate reactive power (simplified)\n        qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            # Set Q proportional to P within limits\n            qg[i] = max(gen['qmin'], min(gen['qmax'], 0.5 * pg_opt[i]))\n\n        solution = {\n            'status': 'optimal',\n            'vm': vm,\n            'va': theta,\n            'pg': pg_opt,\n            'qg': qg,\n            'objective_value': result.fun\n        }\n\n        return solution\n    else:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {result.message}\")\n        return {'status': 'infeasible', 'error': result.message}\n\ndef calculate_branch_flows(network_data: dict, solution: dict) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    flows = []\n    vm = solution['vm']\n    va = solution['va']\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        # Calculate voltages\n        V_f = vm[f_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n        V_t = vm[t_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n        # Apply transformer\n        if tap != 1.0:\n V_f) / z\n        S_to = V_t * np.conj(I_t)\n\n        s_from_mva = np.abs(S_from)\n        s_to_mva = np.abs(S_to)\n\n        loading = max(s_from_mva, s_to_mva) / limit * 100 if limit > 0 else 0\n\n        flows.append({\n            'from_bus': br['f_bus'],\n            'to_bus': br['t_bus'],\n            'pf_from': np.real(S_from),\n            'qf_from': np.imag(S_from),\n max_voltage_violation = 0.0\n    max_branch_overload = 0.0\n\n    # Check power balance\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF using linear approximation\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    gencost = (pg)\n    var_idx = {f'pg_{i}': i for i in range(n_gens)}\n    n_vars = n_gens\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n (susceptance matrix)\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        x = br['br_x']\n        if x == 0:\n            x = 0.01  # Avoid division by zero\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        B[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += b\n        B[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += b\n        B[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= b\n        B[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= b\n\n    # Remove reference bus row/column to make it invertible\n    B_reduced = np.delete(np.delete(B, ref_bus_idx, axis=0), ref_bus_idx, axis=1)\n\n    # Power balance constraints\n    def power_balance(x):\n        \"\"\"Power balance at each bus (except reference)\"\"\"\n pg_at_bus[bus_idx] = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n\n        # Load at each bus\n        pd_at_bus = np.array([bus['pd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Net injection (generation - load)\n flow\n        try:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, net_injection_reduced)\n        except:\n            # If singular, use pseudo-inverse\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.pinv(B_reduced) @ net_injection_reduced\n\n        # Reconstruct full angle vector\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[:ref_bus_idx] = theta_reduced[:ref_bus_idx]\n        theta[ref_bus_idx + 1:] = theta_reduced[ref_bus_idx:]\n\n        # Power mismatch at each bus (should be ~0)\n        mismatches = []\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i == ref_bus_idx:\n                continue\n\n            P_calc = 0.0\n            for j in range(n_buses):\n                if i == j:\n                    continue\n                P_calc += B[i, j] * (theta[i] - theta[j])\n\n            mismatch = P_calc - (pg_at_bus[i] - pd_at_bus[i])\n            mismatches.append(mismatch)\n\n        return np.array(mismatches)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    def branch_flow_limits(x):\n        \"\"\"Calculate branch flow violations\"\"\"\n        # Generator injection at each bus\n        pg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen['gen_bus']]\n            pg_at_bus[bus_idx] = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n\n        # Load at each bus\n        pd_at_bus = np.array([bus['pd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Net injection\n        net_injection = pg_at_bus - pd_at_bus\n\n        # Solve for angles\n        try:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx))\n        except:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.pinv(B_reduced) @ np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx)\n\n        theta = violations = []\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n            t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n            x = br['br_x']\n            if x == 0:\n                x = 0.01\n\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            flow = b * (theta[f_bus_idx] - theta[t_bus_idx])\n            limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n            violations.append(limit - abs(flow))\n\n        return np.array(violations)\n\n    # Objective function\n    def objective(x):\n        \"\"\"Minimize generation cost\"\"\"\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            if gen['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n            cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n            if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n                c2, c1, c0 = {total_pmin:.2f} - {total_pmax:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n\n    if total_pmax < total_load:\n        print(f\"WARNING: Total generation capacity ({total_pmax:.2f} MW) MW\")\n\n    # Solve using simple linear programming (since DC-OPF is linear)\n    from scipy.optimize import linprog\n\n    # Objective coefficients (linear approximation)\n    c = np.array([gencost[i]['cost'][-2] if len(gencost[i]['cost']) >= 2 else 1.0\n                  for i in range(n_gens)])\n\n    # Equality constraints: power balance\n    A_eq = []\n    b_eq = []\n\n    # Build power balance matrix\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        if bus_idx == ref_bus_idx:\n            continue\n\n        row = np.zeros(n_gens)\n        for gen_idx, gen in = np.array(A_eq)\n    b_eq = np.array(b_eq)\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = [(gen['pmin'], gen['pmax']) for gen in generators]\n\n    # Solve linear program\n    result = linprog(c, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs',\n                     options={'disp': True})\n\n    if result.success:\n        # Calculate full solution\n        pg_opt = result.x\n\n        # Calculate angles\n        pg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen['gen_bus']]\n            pg_at_bus[bus_idx] = pg_opt[i]\n\n        pd_at_bus = np.array([bus['pd'] for bus in buses])\n        net_injection = pg_at_bus - pd_at_bus\n\n        try:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx))\n        except:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.pinv(B_reduced) @ np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx)\n\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[:ref_bus_idx] = max_voltage_violation = 0.0\n    max_branch_overload = 0.0\n\n    # Check power balance\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if \"\"\"\n    Solve DC-OPF using linear approximation\n    \"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    gencost = (pg)\n    var_idx = {f'pg_{i}': i for i in range(n_gens)}\n    n_vars = n_gens\n\n    # Initial guess\n    x0 = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        x0[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']] = max(gen['pg'], (gen['pmin'] + gen['pmax']) / 2)\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = [(gen['pmin'], gen['pmax']) for gen in generators]\n\n    # Build B matrix for DC power flow (susceptance matrix)\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        x = br['br_x']\n        if x == 0:\n            x = 0.01  # Avoid division by zero\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n        B[f_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] += b\n        B[t_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] += b\n        B[f_bus_idx, t_bus_idx] -= b\n        B[t_bus_idx, f_bus_idx] -= b\n\n    # Remove reference bus row/column to make it invertible\n    B_reduced = np.delete(np.delete(B, ref_bus_idx, axis=0), ref_bus_idx, axis=1)\n\n    # Power balance constraints\n    def power_balance(x):\n        \"\"\"Power balance at each bus (except reference)\"\"\"\n        # Generator injection at each bus\n        pg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen['gen_bus']]\n            pg_at_bus[bus_idx] = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n\n        # Load at each bus\n        pd_at_bus = np.array([bus['pd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Net injection (generation - load)\n        net_injection = pg_at_bus - pd_at_bus\n\n        # Remove reference bus\n        net_injection_reduced = np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx)\n\n        # Calculate angle differences from DC power flow\n        try:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, net_injection_reduced)\n        except:\n            # If singular, use pseudo-inverse\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.pinv(B_reduced) @ net_injection_reduced\n\n        # Reconstruct full angle vector\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[:ref_bus_idx] = theta_reduced[:ref_bus_idx]\n        theta[ref_bus_idx + 1:] = theta_reduced[ref_bus_idx:]\n\n        # Power mismatch at each bus (should be ~0)\n        mismatches = []\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i == ref_bus_idx:\n                continue\n\n            P_calc = 0.0\n            for j in range(n_buses):\n                if i == j:\n                    continue\n                P_calc += B[i, j] * (theta[i] - theta[j])\n\n            mismatch = P_calc - (pg_at_bus[i] - pd_at_bus[i])\n            mismatches.append(mismatch)\n\n        return np.array(mismatches)\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    def branch_flow_limits(x):\n        \"\"\"Calculate branch flow violations\"\"\"\n        # Generator injection at each bus\n        pg_at_bus = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[gen['gen_bus']]\n            pg_at_bus[bus_idx] = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n\n        # Load at each bus\n        pd_at_bus = np.array([bus['pd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Net injection\n        net_injection = pg_at_bus - pd_at_bus\n\n        # Solve for angles\n        try:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx))\n        except:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.pinv(B_reduced) @ np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx)\n\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[:ref_bus_idx] = theta_reduced[:ref_bus_idx]\n        theta[ref_bus_idx + 1:] = theta_reduced[ref_bus_idx:]\n\n        # Calculate branch flows and violations\n        violations = []\n        for br in branches:\n            if br['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n            t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n            x = br['br_x']\n            if x == 0:\n                x = 0.01\n\n            b = 1.0 / x\n            flow = b * (theta[f_bus_idx] - theta[t_bus_idx])\n            limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n            violations.append(limit - abs(flow))\n\n        return np.array(violations)\n\n    # Objective function\n    def objective(x):\n        \"\"\"Minimize generation cost\"\"\"\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            if gen['status'] != 1:\n                continue\n\n            cost_data = gencost[i] if i < len(gencost) else {'cost': [0, 1, 0]}\n            cost_coeffs = cost_data['cost']\n\n            if len(cost_coeffs) >= 3:\n                c2, c1, c0 = cost_coeffs[-3], cost_coeffs[-2], cost_coeffs[-1]\n            else:\n                c2, c1, c0 = 0, 1, 0\n\n            pg = x[var_idx[f'pg_{i}']]\n            total_cost += c2 * (pg ** 2) + c1 * pg + c0\n\n        return total_cost\n\n    print(f\"Solving DC-OPF with {n_vars} variables...\")\n\n    # Check total generation capacity vs load\n    total_pmin = sum(gen['pmin'] for gen in generators # Build power balance matrix\n    for bus_idx in range(n_buses):\n        if bus_idx == ref_bus_idx:\n            continue\n\n        row = np.zeros(n_gens)\n        for gen_idx, gen in ref_bus_idx))\n        except:\n            theta_reduced = np.linalg.pinv(B_reduced) @ np.delete(net_injection, ref_bus_idx)\n\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[:ref_bus_idx] = theta_reduced[:ref_bus_idx]\n        theta[ref_bus_idx + 1:] = theta_reduced[ref_bus_idx:]\n\n        # Set voltage magnitudes to nominal (1.0 pu for DC approximation)\n        vm = dict) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in va[t_bus_idx])\n\n        # Apply transformer\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            V_f = V_f / tap\n\n        # Calculate power flow\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        I_f = (V_f - V_t) / z\n calculate_branch_flows(network_data: dict, solution: dict) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Calculate branch power flows\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    branches = network_data['branches']\n    bus_id_to_idx = {b['bus_id']: i for i, b in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    flows = []\n    vm = solution['vm']\n    va = solution['va']\n\n    for br in branches:\n        if br['status'] != 1:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['f_bus']]\n        t_bus_idx = bus_id_to_idx[br['t_bus']]\n\n        r = br['br_r']\n        x = br['br_x']\n        b = br['br_b']\n        tap = br['ratio'] if br['ratio'] != 0 else 1.0\n        limit = br['rate_a'] if br['rate_a'] > 0 else 1e6\n\n        # Calculate voltages\n        V_f = vm[f_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[f_bus_idx])\n        V_t = vm[t_bus_idx] * np.exp(1j * va[t_bus_idx])\n\n        # Apply transformer\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            V_f = V_f / tap\n\n        # Calculate power flow\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        I_f = (V_f - V_t) / z\n        S_from = V_f * np.conj(I_f)\n\n        # Reverse direction\n        I_t = (V_t - V_f) / z\n        S_to = V_t * np.conj(I_t)\n\n        s_from_mva = np.abs(S_from)\n br['t_bus'],\n            'pf_from': np.real(S_from),\n            'qf_from': np.imag(S_from),\n            'pt_from': np.real(S_to),\n            'qt_from': np.imag(S_to),\n            's_from_mva': s_from_mva,\n            's_to_mva': s_to_mva,\n            'limit': limit,\n            'loading_pct': loading\n        })\n\n    return flows\n\ndef generate_report(network_data: dict, solution: dict) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate report in required JSON format\"\"\"\n    buses = network_data['buses']\n    generators = network_data['generators']\n\n    total_pg = np.sum(solution['pg'])\n    total_qg = np.sum(solution['qg'])\n    total_pd = np.sum([b['pd'] for b in buses])\n    total_qd = np.sum([b['qd'] for b in buses])\n    total_losses = total_pg - total_pd\n\n    # Calculate enumerate(buses):\n        gen_indices = [idx for idx, g in enumerate(generators) if g['gen_bus'] == bus['bus_id']]\n\n        pg_sum = np.sum([solution['pg'][idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n        qg_sum = np.sum([solution['qg'][idx] for idx in gen_indices])\n\n        mismatch_p = abs(pg_sum - bus['pd'])\n        mismatch_q = abs(qg_sum - bus['qd'])\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, mismatch_p)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, mismatch_q)\n\n        vm = solution['vm'][i]\n        if vm > bus['vmax']:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, vm - bus['vmax'])\n        elif vm < bus['vmin']:\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, bus['vmin'] - vm)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n 'qmin_MVAr': float(gen['qmin']),\n                'qmax_MVAr': float(gen['qmax'])\n            })\n\n    bus_data = []\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        bus_data.append({\n            'id': bus['bus_id'],\n            'vm_pu': float(solution['vm'][i]),\n            'va_deg': float(np.degrees(solution['va'][i])),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus['vmin']),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus['vmax'])\n        })\n\n    most_loaded_branches = []\n    for flow in most_loaded:\n        most_loaded_branches.append({\n            'from_bus': flow['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': flow['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': float(flow['loading_pct']),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(flow['s_from_mva']),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(flow['s_to_mva']),\n            'limit_MVA': float(flow['limit'])\n        })\n\n    solver_status = \"optimal\" if solution['status'] == \"optimal\" else \"infeasible\"\n\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": float(solution['objective_value']) if solution['objective_value'] is not None else 0.0,\n            \"total_load_MW\": float(total_pd),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": float(total_qd),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": float(total_pg),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": float(total_qg),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": float(total_losses),\n
